					}

						add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
							type = infantry_equipment
							amount = 100
							producer = FRA
						}
					}
					add_to_variable = {
						global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 16
						tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
					}
					# Always award TotalAntiCommunistScore for anti-communist actions
					add_to_variable = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaTotalAntiCommunistScore = 2
					}
					# Award faction-specific score based on actor
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_in_array = {
								FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = ROOT.id
							}
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = 2
							tooltip = Add_to_French_Victory_Points_TT
						}
					}

						limit = {
							is_in_array = {
								FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = ROOT.id
							}
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependenceScore = 2
							tooltip = Add_to_Independent_Victory_Points_TT
						}
					}
					else_if = {

							}

							}
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintangScore = 20
							tooltip = Add_to_Kuomintang_Victory_Points_TT
						}
					}

					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.35
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
				}
				# Other unit effects
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 500
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_paradrop_critical_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
		}

				base = 0.07
			}

					reference = 0.5
				}

							is_in_array = {
								FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = victim_country
							}
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_B_Points = 165
							tooltip = Add_to_De_Escalation_Phase_TT
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								is_in_array = {
									FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = ROOT.id
								}
							}
							add_to_variable = {
								FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnicScore = 30
								tooltip = Add_to_Ethnic_Victory_Points_TT
							}
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = {
								is_in_array = {
									FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = ROOT.id
								}
							}
							add_to_variable = {
								FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = 30
								tooltip = Add_to_French_Victory_Points_TT
							}

								}
							}
							add_to_variable = {
								FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependenceScore = 30
								tooltip = Add_to_Independent_Victory_Points_TT
							}

					}

						limit = {
							is_in_array = {

							}

								}
							}
							add_to_variable = {
								FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnicScore = 30
								tooltip = Add_to_Ethnic_Victory_Points_TT
							}

									FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = ROOT.id
								}
							}
							add_to_variable = {
								FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependenceScore = 30
								tooltip = Add_to_Independent_Victory_Points_TT
							}
						}
					}
					else = {
					}
				}
				# Other unit effects
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 500
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_paradrop_critical_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
		}
		success_factors = {
			disaster = {
				base = 0.075
			}
			success = {
				base = 0.25
				# Raiding unit modifiers
				experience = {
					weight = 0.1
					start_weight = -0.05
					reference = 0.5
				}
				organisation = {
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05
					reference = 100
				}
				strength = {
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05
					reference = 1
				}
				recon = {
					weight = 0.08
					reference = 10
				}
				### Target Modifiers
				resistance = {
					reference = 100
					start_reference = 20					# Resistance below 20 doesn't help
					weight = 0.4
				}
				air_superiority = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.25
					start_weight = -0.25
				}
				anti_air = {
					reference = 0
					weight = 0
				}
				interception = {
					reference = 0
					weight = 0
				}

					reference = 2					# More than 2 DIVs in the target Province shouldn't make any difference
					weight = -0.5

					weight = 0.2
					#start_reference = 50
					reference = 100
				}
			}

				base = 0.05
			}

	raid_supply_node_commie = {
		category = insurgency_raids_state
		days_to_prepare = 14
		custom_map_icon = GFX_raid_type_icon_other_raid
		target_icon = GFX_other_target_icon
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 20
			# Reduce raid priority for VIE when at war and surrender limit > 0%
			# Prevents VIE from allocating too many units to raids during active combat
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				tag = VIE
				has_war = yes
				surrender_progress > 0
			}
		}
		command_power = 1
		allowed = {
		}
		visible = {

			NOT = {

			country_exists = VIE

			#Immediate Does Not Require War
		}

				FROM = {

	light_bombing_raid_commie = {
		days_to_prepare = 20
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 2
		}
		category = insurgency_raids
		command_power = 5
		allowed = {
		}
		# Raid Type is visible
		visible = {
			is_in_array = {
				FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = ROOT.id
			}
			check_variable = {
				global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 5
			}
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
		}
		# Specific Target is visible
		show_target = {
			OR = {
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = FROM.id
					}
				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = FROM.id
					}
				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = FROM.id
					}
				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = FROM.id
					}
				}
			}
			var:target_state = {
				OR = {
					is_core_of = VIN
					is_core_of = CAM
					is_core_of = LOS
				}
			}
		}
		# Can prepare
		available = {
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
			country_exists = VIN
			country_exists = VIE
		}
		launchable = {
		}
		target_type = {
			province = any
		}
		custom_map_icon = GFX_raid_type_icon_other_raid
		target_icon = GFX_other_target_icon
		arrow = {
			type = air
		}
		#unit_model = {
		#    type = equipment
		#}
		unit_requirements = {
			equipment = {
				type = {
					air_transport
				}
				amount = {
					min = 24
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			equipment = {
				type = {
					cas
				}
				amount = {
					min = 24
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			equipment = {
				type = {
					fighter
				}
				amount = {
					min = 24
				}
			}
		}
		starting_point = {
			types = {
				air_base
			}
		}
		success_factors = {
			success = {
				base = 0.65
				# Raiding unit modifiers
				experience = {
					weight = 0.2
					start_weight = -0.2
					reference = 1
				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 100					# As air unit agility approaches 100 (as defined by the value above), increase chance by this value
					weight = 0.1					# At 0 agility, the modifier will have negative effect.
					start_weight = -0.1					# The modifier will scale linearly, meaning at 50 agility, the modifier will be 0.
				}
				reliability = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.1
				}
				air_defence = {
					reference = 100
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05
				}
				air_superiority = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.1
					start_weight = -0.1
				}
				### Target Modifiers
				anti_air = {
					reference = 5
					weight = -0.25
				}
				interception = {
					reference = 500					# More than 500 fighters won't really make any difference
					weight = -0.2
				}
				intel = {
					weight = 0.3
					#start_reference = 50
					reference = 100
				}
			}
			critical = {
				base = 0.15
			}
		}
		success_levels = {
			failure = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
						damage_units = {
							province = var:ROOT.target_province
							damage = 0.05
							org_damage = 0.05
							str_damage = 0.05
							ratio = yes
						}
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						plane_loss = 0.4
						ratio = yes
					}
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.05
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_in_array = {
								FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = victim_country
							}
						}
						add_to_variable = {

						}
						add_to_variable = {

							tooltip = Add_to_Kuomintang_Victory_Points_TT

					}
					else_if = {

							tooltip = Add_to_Independent_Victory_Points_TT
						}
					}

							is_in_array = {

							}
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_B_Points = 80
							tooltip = Add_to_De_Escalation_Phase_TT
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = 10
							tooltip = Add_to_French_Victory_Points_TT
						}
					}

							}

							FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnicScore = 10

						}
					}
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "raid_failed_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			limited_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
						damage_units = {
							province = var:ROOT.target_province
							damage = 0.1
							org_damage = 0.1
							str_damage = 0.1
							ratio = yes
						}
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {

		days_to_prepare = 20
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 2
		}
		category = insurgency_raids
		command_power = 5
		allowed = {
		}
		# Raid Type is visible
		visible = {
			OR = {
				is_in_array = {
					FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = ROOT.id
				}
				is_in_array = {
					FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = ROOT.id
				}
				is_in_array = {
					FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = ROOT.id
				}
			}
			check_variable = {
				global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 5
			}
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
		}
		# Specific Target is visible
		show_target = {
			OR = {
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = FROM.id
					}
				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = FROM.id
					}
				}
			}
			var:target_state = {
				OR = {
					is_core_of = VIN
					is_core_of = CAM
					is_core_of = LOS
				}
			}
		}
		# Can prepare
		available = {
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
			country_exists = VIN
			country_exists = VIE
		}

		}

		}

				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 100					# As air unit agility approaches 100 (as defined by the value above), increase chance by this value
					weight = 0.1					# At 0 agility, the modifier will have negative effect.

	tactical_bombing_raid_commie = {
		days_to_prepare = 20
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 2
		}
		category = insurgency_raids
		command_power = 5
		allowed = {
		}
		# Raid Type is visible
		visible = {
			is_in_array = {
				FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = ROOT.id
			}
			check_variable = {
				global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 5
			}
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
		}
		# Specific Target is visible
		show_target = {
			OR = {
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = FROM.id
					}
				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = FROM.id
					}
				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = FROM.id
					}
				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = FROM.id
					}
				}
			}
			var:target_state = {
				OR = {
					is_core_of = VIN
					is_core_of = CAM
					is_core_of = LOS
				}
			}
		}
		# Can prepare
		available = {
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
			country_exists = VIN
			country_exists = VIE
		}
		launchable = {
		}
		target_type = {
			province = any
		}
		custom_map_icon = GFX_raid_type_icon_other_raid
		target_icon = GFX_other_target_icon
		arrow = {
			type = air
		}
		#unit_model = {
		#    type = equipment
		#}
		unit_requirements = {
			equipment = {
				type = {
					tactical_bomber
				}
				amount = {
					min = 24
				}
			}
		}
		starting_point = {
			types = {
				air_base
			}
		}
		success_factors = {
			success = {
				base = 0.65
				# Raiding unit modifiers
				experience = {
					weight = 0.2
					start_weight = -0.2
					reference = 1
				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 100					# As air unit agility approaches 100 (as defined by the value above), increase chance by this value
					weight = 0.1					# At 0 agility, the modifier will have negative effect.
					start_weight = -0.1					# The modifier will scale linearly, meaning at 50 agility, the modifier will be 0.
				}
				reliability = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.1
				}
				air_defence = {
					reference = 100
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05
				}
				air_superiority = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.1
					start_weight = -0.1
				}
				### Target Modifiers
				anti_air = {
					reference = 5
					weight = -0.25
				}
				interception = {
					reference = 500					# More than 500 fighters won't really make any difference
					weight = -0.2
				}
				intel = {
					weight = 0.3
					#start_reference = 50
					reference = 100
				}
			}
			critical = {
				base = 0.15
			}
		}
		success_levels = {
			failure = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
						damage_units = {
							province = var:ROOT.target_province
							damage = 0.05
							org_damage = 0.05
							str_damage = 0.05
							ratio = yes
						}
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						plane_loss = 0.4
						ratio = yes
					}
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.05
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_in_array = {
								FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = victim_country
							}
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 160
							tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintangScore = 20
							tooltip = Add_to_Kuomintang_Victory_Points_TT
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							is_in_array = {
								FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = victim_country
							}
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_B_Points = 160
							tooltip = Add_to_De_Escalation_Phase_TT
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependenceScore = 20
							tooltip = Add_to_Independent_Victory_Points_TT
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							is_in_array = {
								FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = victim_country
							}
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_B_Points = 160
							tooltip = Add_to_De_Escalation_Phase_TT
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = 20
							tooltip = Add_to_French_Victory_Points_TT
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							is_in_array = {
								FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = victim_country
							}
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_B_Points = 160
							tooltip = Add_to_De_Escalation_Phase_TT
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnicScore = 20
							tooltip = Add_to_Ethnic_Victory_Points_TT
						}
					}
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "raid_failed_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			limited_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
						damage_units = {
							province = var:ROOT.target_province
							damage = 0.15
							org_damage = 0.15
							str_damage = 0.15
							ratio = yes
						}
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						plane_loss = 0.05
						ratio = yes
					}
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.1
					add_to_variable = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore = 20
						tooltip = Add_to_Communist_Victory_Points_TT
					}
					add_to_variable = {
						global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 40
						tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
					}
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_1_limited_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}

							province = var:ROOT.target_province
							damage = 0.25
							org_damage = 0.25
							str_damage = 0.25
							ratio = yes
						}
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.2
					add_to_variable = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore = 30
						tooltip = Add_to_Communist_Victory_Points_TT
					}
					add_to_variable = {
						global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 60
						tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
					}
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_1_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			critical_success = {

							province = var:ROOT.target_province
							damage = 0.4
							org_damage = 0.4
							str_damage = 0.4
							ratio = yes
						}
					}

					}
					add_to_variable = {
						global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 30
						tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
					}
				}

					animation = "attack"

							damage = 0.4
							org_damage = 0.4
							str_damage = 0.4
							ratio = yes
						}
					}

							}

						}

						}
						add_to_variable = {
							FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependenceScore = -30
							tooltip = Add_to_Independent_Victory_Points_TT
						}
					}

							}

		}
		# Specific Target is visible
		show_target = {
			OR = {

					is_in_array = {

					}
				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = FROM.id
					}
				}
			}

				OR = {
					is_core_of = VIN
					is_core_of = CAM
					is_core_of = LOS

		}

				air_agility = {
					reference = 100					# As air unit agility approaches 100 (as defined by the value above), increase chance by this value
					weight = 0.1					# At 0 agility, the modifier will have negative effect.

					else_if = {
						limit = {
							is_in_array = {
								FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = victim_country
							}
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 1680
							tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintangScore = 30
							tooltip = Add_to_Kuomintang_Victory_Points_TT
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependenceScore = -30
							tooltip = Add_to_Independent_Victory_Points_TT
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							is_in_array = {
								FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = victim_country
							}
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 1680
							tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintangScore = 30
							tooltip = Add_to_Kuomintang_Victory_Points_TT
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = -30
							tooltip = Add_to_French_Victory_Points_TT
						}
					}
					else_if = {

							is_in_array = {

							}
						}

	civilian_bombing_raid_anti_commie = {
		days_to_prepare = 20
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 2
		}
		category = insurgency_raids
		command_power = 5
		allowed = {
		}
		# Raid Type is visible
		visible = {
			OR = {
				is_in_array = {
					FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = ROOT.id
				}
				is_in_array = {
					FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = ROOT.id
				}
				is_in_array = {
					FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = ROOT.id
				}
			}
			check_variable = {
				global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 5
			}
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
		}
		# Specific Target is visible
		show_target = {
			OR = {
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = FROM.id
					}
				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = FROM.id
					}
				}
			}
			var:target_state = {
				OR = {
					is_core_of = VIN
					is_core_of = CAM
					is_core_of = LOS
				}
			}
		}
		# Can prepare
		available = {
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
			country_exists = VIN
			country_exists = VIE
		}
		launchable = {
		}
		target_type = {
			state = {
				OR = {
					is_core_of = VIN
					is_core_of = CAM
					is_core_of = LOS
				}

		}

				air_base

		success_factors = {
			success = {
				base = 0.65
				# Raiding unit modifiers
				experience = {
					weight = 0.2
					start_weight = -0.2
					reference = 1
				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 100					# As air unit agility approaches 100 (as defined by the value above), increase chance by this value
					weight = 0.1					# At 0 agility, the modifier will have negative effect.
					start_weight = -0.1					# The modifier will scale linearly, meaning at 50 agility, the modifier will be 0.
				}
				reliability = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.1
				}
				air_defence = {
					reference = 100
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05
				}

					reference = 1
					weight = 0.1
					start_weight = -0.1
				}
				### Target Modifiers
				anti_air = {
					reference = 5
					weight = -0.25
				}

					reference = 500					# More than 500 fighters won't really make any difference
					weight = -0.2

					reference = 100

								attacker = Target_State
								defender = defender_state_test
								defender_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
							}
						}
						else = {
							set_temp_variable = {
								defender_state_test = THIS
							}
							every_neighbor_state = {
								limit = {
									set_temp_variable = {
										attacker_state_test = THIS
									}
									has_border_war_between = {
										attacker = attacker_state_test
										defender = defender_state_test
									}
								}
								set_temp_variable = {
									Target_State = THIS
								}
								damage_units = {
									state = target_state
									damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
									org_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
									str_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
									ratio = yes
								}
								set_border_war_data = {
									attacker = Target_State
									defender = defender_state_test
									attacker_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						plane_loss = 0.05
						ratio = yes
					}
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.1
					add_to_variable = {
						global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 5
						tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
					}
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_1_limited_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			success = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
						ROOT = {
							set_variable = {
								Chemical_Damage = 0.1
							}
							PREV = {
								add_province_modifier = {
									static_modifiers = {
										white_phosphorus
									}
									days = 60
									province = {
										all_provinces = yes
									}
								}
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								owner = {
									check_variable = {
										attacker_tag = THIS
									}

										attacker_state = PREV

	napalm_raid_anti_commie = {
		days_to_prepare = 20
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 2
		}
		category = insurgency_raids
		command_power = 5
		allowed = {
		}
		# Raid Type is visible
		visible = {
			has_tech = NBC_research
			OR = {
				is_in_array = {
					FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = ROOT.id
				}
				is_in_array = {
					FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = ROOT.id
				}
				is_in_array = {
					FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = ROOT.id
				}
			}
			check_variable = {
				global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 5
			}
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
		}
		# Specific Target is visible
		show_target = {
			OR = {
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = FROM.id
					}
				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = FROM.id
					}
				}
			}
			var:target_state = {
				has_border_war = yes
				OR = {
					is_core_of = VIN
					is_core_of = CAM
					is_core_of = LOS
				}
			}
		}
		# Can prepare
		available = {
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
			country_exists = VIN
			country_exists = VIE
		}
		launchable = {
		}
		target_type = {
			state = {

					is_core_of = VIN

				}

		}

		arrow = {
			type = air
		}
		#unit_model = {
		#    type = equipment
		#}
		unit_requirements = {
			equipment = {
				type = {
					tactical_bomber
				}
				amount = {
					min = 24
				}
			}
		}
		starting_point = {
			types = {
				air_base
			}
		}
		success_factors = {
			success = {
				base = 0.65
				# Raiding unit modifiers
				experience = {
					weight = 0.2
					start_weight = -0.2
					reference = 1
				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 100					# As air unit agility approaches 100 (as defined by the value above), increase chance by this value
					weight = 0.1					# At 0 agility, the modifier will have negative effect.
					start_weight = -0.1					# The modifier will scale linearly, meaning at 50 agility, the modifier will be 0.
				}
				reliability = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.1
				}
				air_defence = {
					reference = 100
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05
				}
				air_superiority = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.1
					start_weight = -0.1
				}
				### Target Modifiers
				anti_air = {
					reference = 5
					weight = -0.25
				}
				interception = {
					reference = 500					#
					weight = -0.2
				}
				intel = {
					weight = 0.3
					#start_reference = 50
					reference = 100
				}
			}
			critical = {
				base = 0.15
			}
		}
		success_levels = {
			failure = {
				victim_effects = {
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						plane_loss = 0.4
						ratio = yes
					}
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.05
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "raid_failed_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			limited_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
						ROOT = {
							set_variable = {
								Chemical_Damage = 0.3
							}
							set_variable = {
								ROOT.Civilian_Modifier = -0.001
							}
							PREV = {
								add_province_modifier = {
									static_modifiers = {
										napalm
									}
									days = 60
									province = {
										all_provinces = yes
									}
								}
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								owner = {
									check_variable = {
										attacker_tag = THIS
									}
									check_variable = {
										attacker_state = PREV
									}
								}
							}
							damage_units = {
								state = target_state
								damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
								org_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
								str_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
								ratio = yes
							}
							set_border_war_data = {
								attacker = Target_State
								defender = defender_state_test
								defender_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
							}
						}
						else = {
							set_temp_variable = {
								defender_state_test = THIS
							}
							every_neighbor_state = {
								limit = {
									set_temp_variable = {
										attacker_state_test = THIS
									}
									has_border_war_between = {
										attacker = attacker_state_test
										defender = defender_state_test
									}
								}
								set_temp_variable = {
									Target_State = THIS
								}
								damage_units = {
									state = target_state
									damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
									org_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
									str_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
									ratio = yes
								}
								set_border_war_data = {
									attacker = Target_State
									defender = defender_state_test
									attacker_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
								}
							}
						}
						set_variable = {
							civilian_casualties = state_population
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							ROOT.Civilian_Modifier = -0.002
						}
						multiply_variable = {
							civilian_casualties = ROOT.Civilian_Modifier
						}
						add_manpower = civilian_casualties
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						plane_loss = 0.05
						ratio = yes
					}
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.1
					add_to_variable = {
						global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 160
						tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
					}
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_1_limited_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}

						ROOT = {
							set_variable = {
								Chemical_Damage = 0.3
							}

							ROOT.Civilian_Modifier = -0.004
						}

			type = air
		}
		#unit_model = {
		#    type = equipment
		#}
		unit_requirements = {
			equipment = {
				type = {
					tactical_bomber
				}
				amount = {
					min = 24
				}
			}
		}
		starting_point = {
			types = {
				air_base
			}
		}
		success_factors = {
			success = {
				base = 0.65
				# Raiding unit modifiers
				experience = {
					weight = 0.2
					start_weight = -0.2
					reference = 1
				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 100					# As air unit agility approaches 100 (as defined by the value above), increase chance by this value
					weight = 0.1					# At 0 agility, the modifier will have negative effect.
					start_weight = -0.1					# The modifier will scale linearly, meaning at 50 agility, the modifier will be 0.
				}
				reliability = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.1
				}
				air_defence = {
					reference = 100
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05
				}
				air_superiority = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.1
					start_weight = -0.1
				}
				### Target Modifiers
				anti_air = {
					reference = 5
					weight = -0.25
				}
				interception = {
					reference = 500					#
					weight = -0.2
				}
				intel = {
					weight = 0.3
					#start_reference = 50
					reference = 100
				}
			}
			critical = {
				base = 0.15
			}
		}
		success_levels = {
			failure = {
				victim_effects = {
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						plane_loss = 0.4
						ratio = yes
					}
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.05
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "raid_failed_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			limited_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
						ROOT = {
							set_variable = {
								Chemical_Damage = 0.15
							}
							PREV = {
								add_province_modifier = {
									static_modifiers = {
										agent_orange2
									}
									days = 60
									province = {
										all_provinces = yes
									}
								}
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								owner = {
									check_variable = {
										attacker_tag = THIS
									}
									check_variable = {
										attacker_state = PREV
									}
								}
							}
							damage_units = {
								state = target_state
								damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
								org_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
								str_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
								ratio = yes
							}
							set_border_war_data = {
								attacker = Target_State
								defender = defender_state_test
								defender_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
							}
						}
						else = {
							set_temp_variable = {
								defender_state_test = THIS
							}
							every_neighbor_state = {
								limit = {
									set_temp_variable = {
										attacker_state_test = THIS
									}
									has_border_war_between = {
										attacker = attacker_state_test
										defender = defender_state_test
									}
								}
								set_temp_variable = {
									Target_State = THIS
								}
								damage_units = {
									state = target_state
									damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
									org_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
									str_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
									ratio = yes
								}
								set_border_war_data = {
									attacker = Target_State
									defender = defender_state_test
									attacker_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						plane_loss = 0.05
						ratio = yes
					}
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.1
					add_to_variable = {
						global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 5
						tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
					}
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_1_limited_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}

						if = {
							limit = {
								owner = {
									check_variable = {
										attacker_tag = THIS
									}

										attacker_state = PREV
									}
								}
							}

								defender_state_test = THIS

										defender = defender_state_test
									}
								}
								set_temp_variable = {
									Target_State = THIS
								}
								set_border_war_data = {
									attacker = Target_State
									defender = defender_state_test
									defender_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
								}

						}
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {

					}

					add_to_variable = {

					entity = "air_raids_1_limited_success_entity"

			success = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
						ROOT = {
							set_variable = {
								Chemical_Damage = 0.15
							}
							PREV = {
								add_province_modifier = {
									static_modifiers = {
										mustard_gas
									}
									days = 60
									province = {
										all_provinces = yes
									}
								}
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								owner = {
									check_variable = {
										attacker_tag = THIS
									}
									check_variable = {
										attacker_state = PREV
									}
								}
							}
							set_border_war_data = {
								attacker = Target_State
								defender = defender_state_test
								defender_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
							}
						}
						else = {
							set_temp_variable = {
								defender_state_test = THIS
							}
							every_neighbor_state = {
								limit = {
									set_temp_variable = {
										attacker_state_test = THIS
									}
									has_border_war_between = {
										attacker = attacker_state_test
										defender = defender_state_test
									}
								}
								set_temp_variable = {

										mustard_gas
									}
									days = 60
									province = {
										all_provinces = yes
									}
								}
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								owner = {
									check_variable = {
										attacker_tag = THIS
									}
									check_variable = {
										attacker_state = PREV
									}
								}
							}
							set_border_war_data = {
								attacker = Target_State
								defender = defender_state_test
								defender_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
							}
						}
						else = {
							set_temp_variable = {
								defender_state_test = THIS
							}
							every_neighbor_state = {
								limit = {
									set_temp_variable = {
										attacker_state_test = THIS
									}
									has_border_war_between = {
										attacker = attacker_state_test
										defender = defender_state_test
									}
								}
								set_temp_variable = {
									Target_State = THIS
								}
								set_border_war_data = {
									attacker = Target_State
									defender = defender_state_test
									defender_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.35
					add_to_variable = {
						global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 160
						tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
					}
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_1_critical_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
		}
	}
